核心关注（ML × ferro / 凝聚态）

8 篇

本日实质进展集中在把机器学习嵌入高成本电子结构与实验反演：RS-CIDER以非局域模型近似屏蔽杂化泛函，直接针对带隙、局域电荷和氧化还原能；波函数基础模型和机器学习增强DMRG则分别处理断键多参考态与大活性空间激发能。其余工作把等变自编码器用于三维流体降阶、把图像模型用于表面手性判别，并以DFT解析LiₓCoO₂磁态、ZrTe₅反演对称性破缺和1T-TaS₂层间二聚化电荷动力学。对团队而言，可将这些路线迁移到NbOI₂、CrI₃、CrSBr和BaTiO₃，结合equivariant GNN、MACE、NEP与DFT+U建立结构—自旋—极化—激发态的统一数据链。

  1. 01
    文献研究：Decoding Crystallographic Surface Chirality from Real Space and Momentum Space Images by Machine Learning
    Decoding Crystallographic Surface Chirality from Real Space and Momentum Space Images by Machine Learning
    🎯 核心关注📖 arXiv

    💡 亮点：本文建立机器学习框架，从实空间原子结构图和倒空间费米面投影图中识别金属晶体表面的手性。基于ResNet18的模型能够对合成及实验图像进行手性分类，且倒空间图像的分类性能优于实空间图像。

    📐 方法要点：该工作把晶体表面手性转化为可由实验图像直接判别的机器学习分类问题，输入包括实空间表面形貌图以及动量空间衍射或电子结构图像，并以表面台阶—扭结—平台的高Miller指数拓扑作为标签来源。核心价值在于比较两类表征对左右手性的可分辨性，模型应通过旋转、镜像、尺度和噪声增强训练，同时显式区分保持手性的正交变换与翻转手性的镜像变换；摘要未给出具体网络层数、损失函数和训练集规模。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与等变卷积、群对称图网络和扫描隧道显微图像识别相呼应，但目标不是预测总能或局域环境，而是从观测量反推出空间反演相关的手性序参量。对Hongyu Yu和Hongjun Xiang的工作，最直接的连接是把表面结构、非共线自旋和Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya相互作用放进同一对称性框架；CrI₃、CrSBr表面和磁性台阶可进一步测试结构手性与自旋手性的耦合。Xin-Gao Gong和Ji-Hui Yang已有二维材料、界面及缺陷图像数据可作为迁移场景。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可先用DFT和slab结构搜索生成NbOI₂、CrI₃、CrSBr及BaTiO₃极性表面的左右手台阶、缺陷和吸附构型，再用MACE或NEP充分弛豫，输出STM模拟图、低能电子衍射图和动量分辨谱图。训练具备E(2)/E(3)等变约束的多模态网络，镜像样本作为反手性对照；验证应同时采用留出Miller指数、不同表面终止和实验噪声测试，并用DFT自洽电荷、局域磁矩及DMI符号检查模型是否学到真实结构因素而非成像伪影。

  2. 02
    文献研究：An ab initio foundation model of wavefunctions that accurately describes chemical bond breaking
    An ab initio foundation model of wavefunctions that accurately describes chemical bond breaking
    🎯 核心关注📖 arXiv

    💡 亮点：论文提出基于深度神经网络量子蒙特卡洛波函数的基础模型 Orbformer，并在约 22000 个平衡和解离结构上进行预训练。该模型能够较准确地描述化学键断裂和多参考电子结构问题，并降低对每个新体系从头计算的成本。

    📐 方法要点：该方法以大量低成本、可统一生成的波函数数据预训练基础模型，再对具体分子或反应路径进行微调，从而近似多参考波函数在断键过程中的非平凡节点结构和电子重排。模型输入应包含原子种类、三维坐标、轨道或基组相关表示，输出波函数、能量及可导性质；训练重点是跨分子、键长扫描和不同自旋态的联合损失，而不是只拟合平衡构型。摘要强调可迁移性，但未披露网络架构、基组、预训练样本量及误差分解。

    🔗 相关工作关联：其物理定位接近机器学习Kohn–Sham哈密顿量、神经网络波函数和量子化学势能面，但关键差别是学习对象直接承载电子反对称性与断键时的多参考特征，而非单一绝热势能面。Hongyu Yu和Hongjun Xiang发展的等变哈密顿量、有效模型与对称性约束可提供结构编码；Ji-Hui Yang关注GW-BSE和缺陷激发态，Weibin Chu关注非绝热动力学，两者都需要可靠的激发态和电荷重排表示。与DMRG增强路线相比，本工作更强调跨体系预训练。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可将预训练对象换成BaTiO₃、NbOI₂及CrI₃极化翻转或自旋翻转路径上的局域波函数，数据由DFT+U、杂化泛函和小胞高精度多参考计算共同标定。对每个结构采集轨道、密度矩阵、自旋密度、极化和力，并按键长、畴壁距离、缺陷电荷及磁序分层留出测试集；在长程分子动力学中用能量、力、Berry相位极化、交换参数和自旋翻转能逐项验证，重点检查模型能否处理带电缺陷、近简并磁态与软模区域。

    阅读原文 ↗
  3. 03
    利用机器学习改进密度矩阵重正化群计算，实现快速且准确的激发能
    Fast and Accurate Excitation Energies from Density Matrix Renormalization Group Calculations Improved by Machine Learning
    🎯 核心关注📖 arXiv

    📄 摘要：估计光电性质是现代功能材料科学中的关键需求，也是持续存在的挑战。这需要准确获得具有复杂电子结构体系的基态和激发态，而对于这类体系，精确的多体方法通常计算成本过高。在我们先前关于基态的工作基础上，我们提出一种高效且经济的方法，用于评估功能材料中的电子激发；该方法将密度矩阵重正化群方法作为完备活性空间求解器，并与机器学习技术相结合。我们在π电子相关体系，即多环芳香化合物上展示了该方法的性能。我们还在若干具有挑战性的实例上证明了所得到的机器学习模型具有可迁移性和有效性，这些实例最多包含34个π电子。

    💡 亮点：本文将密度矩阵重正化群方法作为完备活性空间求解器与机器学习结合，用于高效、低成本地计算功能材料中的电子激发。针对多环芳香化合物的π电子相关体系，研究展示了该方法的性能，并在最多包含34个π电子的困难实例中验证了机器学习模型的可迁移性和有效性。

    📐 方法要点：文章把DMRG作为完备活性空间求解器，再用机器学习学习从分子结构、活性空间轨道特征或低成本电子结构描述到DMRG激发能的映射，以减少每个新构型都进行高成本张量网络优化的次数。训练样本来自多环芳香化合物及其π电子体系，重点覆盖不同环数、几何构型、激发类型和最多34个π电子的困难实例；需要对基态与激发态能量差采用统一归一化、分层交叉验证并检查外推误差，摘要未明确模型架构和DMRG截断参数。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与Δ-learning、主动学习势能面和机器学习电子结构校正相似，区别是标签来自DMRG而非单粒子DFT或经验激发模型，因而能保留强关联π体系中的多组态效应。Ji-Hui Yang已有GW-BSE、电子—声子耦合和激发态研究，可比较机器学习DMRG与BSE在局域激发、双激发和电荷转移态上的差异；Weibin Chu的非绝热动力学需要这种低成本激发能接口。Hongyu Yu的波函数/哈密顿量学习则适合进一步预测跃迁密度而非只有标量能量。

    💡 对你方向的启示：团队可把训练集扩展到铁电和二维材料中的局域激发：用小胞DMRG或选定多参考方法计算BaTiO₃氧空位、NbOI₂极性畸变、CrI₃/CrSBr配体场激发的基态—激发态能隙，再以DFT+U、杂化泛函和局域轨道占据作为输入。采用按材料家族和缺陷构型留出的外推测试，验证激发能、振子强度、能量梯度及自旋多重度；将模型嵌入非绝热分子动力学，比较热载流子弛豫时间、态间跃迁概率与小规模高精度基准。

    阅读原文 ↗
  4. 04
    文献研究：Surrogate Modeling of 3D Rayleigh-Benard Convection with Equivariant Autoencoders
    Surrogate Modeling of 3D Rayleigh-Benard Convection with Equivariant Autoencoders
    🎯 核心关注📖 arXiv

    💡 亮点：arXiv:2505.13569v3 Announce Type: replace-cross Abstract: The use of machine learning for modeling, understanding, and controlling large-scale physics systems is quickly gaining in popularity, with ex…

    📐 方法要点：该工作针对三维Rayleigh–Bénard对流构建保留空间对称性的等变自编码器，用高维速度、温度和压力场压缩到低维潜变量，再由解码器重建瞬时流场，并以潜空间动力学或代理模型预测长期演化。关键不是普通卷积降维，而是让平移、旋转、反射等变换在编码和解码前后保持一致，从而减少数据增强负担并保留涡结构几何；训练应覆盖不同Rayleigh数、Prandtl数和边界条件，但摘要未给出具体潜变量维度、网格分辨率与误差指标。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与神经算子、POD/DMD、流体代理模型和物理信息神经网络相关，但等变自编码器更适合表达旋转或镜像相关的场结构，且可在低维空间执行控制和参数扫描。对团队而言，方法层面对应Hongjun Xiang的有效哈密顿量和蒙特卡洛降阶，也对应Xin-Gao Gong的机器学习势与主动学习；Weibin Chu的非绝热动力学可借鉴其长时间轨迹压缩。与材料GNN相比，它处理的是连续三维场而非原子图。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可将输入场改为BaTiO₃极化翻转、畴壁迁移或CrSBr自旋—晶格动力学中的三维时空场：由NEP或MACE生成大尺度分子动力学轨迹，再以局域极化、应变、磁矩、温度和应力网格化。用E(3)等变编码器压缩轨迹，并将外场、电场方向、应变和温度作为条件变量；按材料、尺寸和驱动频率留出测试，验证极化开关时间、畴壁速度、能量守恒、相关长度及功率谱，同时与直接MD和有效哈密顿量蒙特卡洛结果比较。

    阅读原文 ↗
  5. 05
    文献研究：RS-CIDER: A non-local machine learning model for approximating screened hybrid functionals
    RS-CIDER: A non-local machine learning model for approximating screened hybrid functionals
    🎯 核心关注📖 arXiv

    💡 亮点：该论文提出RS-CIDER非局域机器学习交换泛函，用于近似HSE06中的短程Hartree-Fock交换，并能自洽地计算分子和周期性固体。结果表明其可较好复现HSE06的带隙、局域磁性、极化子局域化和中性缺陷能，并有望显著降低计算成本。

    📐 方法要点：RS-CIDER旨在以非局域机器学习模型近似屏蔽杂化泛函中的Hartree–Fock交换贡献，输入不是单一原子邻域，而是由电子密度、梯度、轨道或密度矩阵构造的局域描述与有限范围外的环境信息，从而表达屏蔽交换的空间非局域性。训练标签应来自HSE06等屏蔽杂化计算，目标包括交换能、势或能量差，并通过周期性体系、不同带隙和电荷局域程度的数据覆盖提升可迁移性；摘要未给出截断半径、网络规模和自洽收敛细节。

    🔗 相关工作关联：该路线直接连接机器学习交换—相关泛函、CIDER类密度泛函表示、机器学习Kohn–Sham哈密顿量及神经网络势，但与普通半局域DFT回归相比，核心难点是长程交换和自洽势的一致性。Hongyu Yu和Hongjun Xiang已有机器学习哈密顿量、张量性质与可迁移模型经验，可用于把RS-CIDER从能量校正扩展到矩阵元；Ji-Hui Yang和Xin-Gao Gong关注带隙、缺陷和输运，正是HSE成本最显著的材料场景。LiₓCoO₂还可检验强关联限制。

    💡 对你方向的启示：建议建立BaTiO₃、NbOI₂、CrI₃、CrSBr及SiC缺陷的PBE、PBE+U、HSE06和必要的自旋轨道耦合数据集，按结构、磁序、缺陷电荷和应变分层采样。先训练能量与势的联合模型，再把预测势嵌入自洽Kohn–Sham循环；验证应包括带隙、极化、Born有效电荷、交换分裂、缺陷形成能、磁交换常数和声子频率，并与直接HSE及GW-BSE交叉比较，特别检查反铁磁、非共线磁性和局域极化子。

    阅读原文 ↗
  6. 06
    文献研究：Assessing the magnetic states and the accuracy of first-principles Hubbard corrections for the battery cathode LiₓCoO₂ (x=0,1)
    Assessing the magnetic states and the accuracy of first-principles Hubbard corrections for the battery cathode LiₓCoO₂ (x=0,1)
    🎯 核心关注📖 arXiv

    💡 亮点：研究利用密度泛函理论结合自洽Hubbard修正和长程范德华相互作用，系统分析不同锂含量LiₓCoO2正极材料的结构、电子和磁性状态。结果表明，Hubbard投影子的选择会显著影响电子结构，前沿Wannier函数投影能够改善计算描述。

    📐 方法要点：本文系统比较LiCoO₂与脱锂CoO₂在不同磁态和Hubbard U处理下的结构、电子、磁性及电化学性质，重点评估第一性原理Hubbard修正能否同时描述层间作用、自相互作用误差、Co价态变化和复杂磁性能量面。技术路线应包括多种共线或非共线磁序、结构弛豫、U值扫描、可能的自旋轨道耦合以及形成能和电压比较；摘要未给出采用的交换—相关泛函、U确定方案、超胞大小和具体磁序清单。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与DFT+U、杂化泛函、线性响应U、团簇展开和自旋哈密顿量构建直接相关，也延续Ji-Hui Yang关于高脱锂LiCoO₂退化与电池界面的研究。Hongjun Xiang和Hongyu Yu的有效哈密顿量、复杂磁相互作用与机器学习势可用于把离散磁态扩展到大尺寸脱锂无序；Xin-Gao Gong关注缺陷、扩散和输运，可进一步处理氧空位与锂空位耦合。与RS-CIDER相比，本工作提供了强关联电池正极的关键基准。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可将LiₓCoO₂数据扩展为x=0、0.25、0.5、0.75、1及不同锂空位排布，使用线性响应U、HSE和实验晶格常数共同校准，并训练带磁性和化学环境输入的MACE或NEP势。随后以DFT+U标定Co—O交换、氧空位形成能、迁移势垒和局域自旋，结合团簇展开与蒙特卡洛预测温度和浓度效应；验证电压曲线、晶格参数、磁矩、XAS/中子可比特征及高脱锂相的结构稳定性。

    阅读原文 ↗
  7. 07
    文献研究：Revealing Hidden Inversion Symmetry Breaking in ZrTe₅ via Phonon-Assisted Heterodyne Amplification
    Revealing Hidden Inversion Symmetry Breaking in ZrTe₅ via Phonon-Assisted Heterodyne Amplification
    🎯 核心关注📖 arXiv

    💡 亮点：论文利用太赫兹场诱导二次谐波和声子辅助异频放大，探测了通常难以观测的ZrTe5平衡态反演对称性破缺。实验表明体系存在沿晶体a轴的体极性畸变，并且该畸变可在室温下保持，而c轴方向未显示相同特征。

    📐 方法要点：该工作利用声子辅助异频放大把极弱的反演对称性破缺信号从强背景中分离出来：通过相干激发特定红外或拉曼活性声子，将待测非线性极化与参考场进行异频混频，并用偏振、频率和相位分辨的非线性光谱追踪ZrTe₅的隐藏对称性降低。方法上需要结合群论选择定则、晶格动力学和第一性原理振动模式计算，将信号分解为平衡结构允许项与声子诱导项；摘要未给出脉冲时序、声子频率和拟合模型。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与非线性声子学、太赫兹驱动结构控制、二次谐波产生和瞬态反射谱相连，优势是借助相干参考放大小的反演破缺，而不是仅依赖静态衍射平均结构。Hongjun Xiang关注极性、铁电和群论分析，BaTiO₃与NbOI₂可直接作为对称性基准；Weibin Chu的非绝热动力学可描述声子驱动后的电子响应，Xin-Gao Gong可用机器学习势延长声子—晶格演化。其核心与传统静态DFT判定中心对称不同。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可对CrSBr、CrI₃和NbOI₂构建含层间滑移、磁序、应变及缺陷的声子数据库，用DFT+U加自旋轨道耦合计算振动本征矢、Born电荷、二阶和三阶极化响应。以MACE或NEP生成受激声子长时间轨迹，再由实时TDDFT或机器学习Kohn–Sham模型计算异频信号；验证实验可测的偏振选择定则、相位翻转、温度依赖和磁场依赖，并用反演对称恢复的对照结构排除表面或应变伪信号。

    阅读原文 ↗
  8. 08
    文献研究：Unconventional anisotropic charge dynamics in bulk 1T-TaS₂ induced by interlayer dimerization
    Unconventional anisotropic charge dynamics in bulk 1T-TaS₂ induced by interlayer dimerization
    🎯 核心关注📖 arXiv

    💡 亮点：该工作结合温度和偏振分辨红外光谱与密度泛函理论，研究块体1T-TaS₂电荷密度波相中的各向异性电荷动力学。结果表明，层内响应主要由Ta层的结构重构决定，而层间响应由星形畸变层之间的准一维二聚化主导，并认为该二聚化是金属-绝缘体转变的关键机制。

    📐 方法要点：该研究结合温度和偏振分辨红外光谱，比较块体1T-TaS₂中平行层面与垂直层面的电荷动力学，并用DFT解释层间二聚化如何改变电荷响应的各向异性。计算路线需要从低温commensurate CDW结构出发，显式包含层间堆垛、Ta原子星状畸变和层间二聚键，计算能带、振子矩阵元、介电函数及可能的Drude或Lorentz响应；摘要未说明是否采用自旋轨道耦合、电子关联修正及具体超胞。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与电荷密度波、层间杂化、极化红外响应和二维范德华材料输运直接相关，区别在于把通常被视为层间弱耦合的二聚化作为决定电荷动力学方向性的结构变量。Hongjun Xiang和Hongyu Yu关于层间耦合、有效哈密顿量及机器学习哈密顿量的经验可用于构建CDW—电子模型；Xin-Gao Gong和Ji-Hui Yang已有电子—声子耦合、Wannier输运和二维材料研究，Weibin Chu则可延伸到光激发后的非平衡电荷弛豫。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可先用DFT或DFT+U生成不同层间滑移、二聚强度、CDW相位和应变下的1T-TaS₂结构，利用Wannier化提取层内、层间跃迁与电荷序参数，再训练具备旋转和层交换对称性的等变模型。以MACE或NEP模拟温度、缺陷和光激发诱导的结构涨落，结合Boltzmann输运或实时电子动力学预测方向分辨电导；验证红外频率、谱权重、散射率、层间/层内电流比，并与解耦层模型和实验温度扫描比较。

  2. 02
    无对称性的自旋劈裂：近补偿亚铁磁体与交替磁性的起源
    📖 arXiv📅 2026-09-14🎯 相关度 9

    📝 简单总结：论文研究无对称性约束的近补偿亚铁磁体中的自旋劈裂，并重新讨论交替磁性的起源。作者指出，晶体对称性主要负责组织或禁止自旋劈裂，而真正决定劈裂的是磁性轨道及配体的各向异性排列。

    🔗 与我们工作的关系：与团队关注的交替磁性、非共线或复杂磁性、磁性拓扑性质、对称性分析和自旋哈密顿量高度直接相关。其关于轨道-配体几何、局域磁性描述符与自旋劈裂关系的观点，可用于改进交替磁材料筛选和机器学习模型的物理特征设计。

    🔗 与我们工作的关系：与我们的锂离子电池材料、第一性原理计算、磁性材料和Wannier方法方向高度相关。其自洽U参数、Co-O间位相互作用及磁态系统搜索方法，可用于改进电池正极和固态电解质的电子结构与磁性建模。

    🔗 与我们工作的关系：与我们的第一性原理计算、机器学习Kohn-Sham哈密顿量、材料软件、大尺度缺陷模拟和锂电池界面方向高度相关。线性标度DFT可弥补传统DFT在复杂界面、晶界、动态缺陷和长程结构上的尺寸限制。

    📖 arXiv📅 2026-09-14🎯 相关度 8

    📖 arXiv📅 2026-09-14🎯 相关度 8

    📝 简单总结：该工作提出基于几何规则的高通量筛选算法，用于识别常规蜂窝、超蜂窝、阶梯或锯齿蜂窝以及三维蜂窝结构。研究还进一步筛选可剥离二维材料、磁性蜂窝体系和具有电子不稳定性的候选材料，并建立了相应数据库。

    🔗 与我们工作的关系：与团队的高通量计算、晶体结构识别、二维材料、磁性材料、电子结构分析和逆向材料设计直接相关。其几何过滤与可剥离性、磁性及态密度峰值指标相结合的策略，适合扩展为面向特定物性目标的材料筛选流程。

    💡 进一步工作建议：可在几何筛选后加入第一性原理磁性、拓扑性质、声子稳定性和形成能计算，进一步评估候选材料的实验可合成性。还可结合图神经网络或主动学习，预测蜂窝材料的磁交换、带隙和非线性光学响应。

    📖 arXiv📅 2026-09-14🎯 相关度 5

    📝 简单总结：本文在广义含时Ginzburg-Landau框架下研究连接两个热库的II型超导体中超导畴壁的非平衡动力学，并将序参量与准粒子分布作为耦合动力学变量。结果表明，温度偏置驱动畴壁向高温边界运动，局部动量平衡中同时出现黏滞力和序参量—准粒子分布耦合产生的力。

    🔗 与我们工作的关系：与我们的超导、畴壁动力学、非平衡过程和有效模型方向相关，但方法主要是连续场论和含时Ginzburg-Landau模型，和团队常用的第一性原理、自旋哈密顿量及原子尺度模拟联系较弱。其热偏置驱动畴壁运动和耗散机制可为研究磁畴壁、铁电畴壁及热输运耦合提供理论启发。

    💡 进一步工作建议：可将该非平衡畴壁理论与微观电子结构、电子—声子耦合和分子动力学相结合，计算材料特异的阻尼与输运参数。还可比较热梯度驱动超导畴壁、铁电畴壁和磁畴壁的统一动力学机制。

  30. 30
    文献研究：Decoding Crystallographic Surface Chirality from Real Space and Momentum Space Images by Machine Learning
    📖 arXiv📅 2026-09-14🎯 相关度 4

    📝 简单总结：本文建立机器学习框架，从实空间原子结构图和倒空间费米面投影图中识别金属晶体表面的手性。基于ResNet18的模型能够对合成及实验图像进行手性分类，且倒空间图像的分类性能优于实空间图像。

    🔗 与我们工作的关系：与我们的机器学习、材料信息学、晶体结构和对称性分析方向有一定关联，也涉及自旋电子学潜在应用，但并未直接研究第一性原理电子结构模拟、磁性或自旋相互作用。其跨表征域图像学习和实验图像泛化思路可借鉴。

    💡 进一步工作建议：可将等变神经网络或基于群论的模型用于手性、磁性空间群和自旋纹理识别，并结合第一性原理计算构建结构—电子态—输运性质数据集。进一步可研究表面手性对自旋轨道耦合、Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用和催化反应的影响。

  31. 31
    文献研究：Quantitative and bond-traceable resonant X-ray optical tensors of organic molecules
    📖 arXiv📅 2026-09-14🎯 相关度 4

    📝 简单总结：论文利用无监督机器学习聚类密度泛函理论计算，识别有机分子中的主要电子跃迁，并结合角分辨吸收实验构建具有化学键和取向分辨率的共振 X 射线光学张量。该模型能够描述不同取向和晶体堆积样品的光学响应。

    🔗 与我们工作的关系：与团队的机器学习、电子结构计算、光学响应和界面光电性质研究存在较明显的方法联系。其将机器学习、DFT 电子跃迁与实验光谱结合的策略，可借鉴到卤化物钙钛矿、二维半导体和异质结的各向异性光学研究中。

    💡 进一步工作建议：建议将类似方法扩展到含缺陷、应变和层间耦合的二维材料及钙钛矿异质结构，解析局域化学环境对光学跃迁和电荷转移的影响。

    文献研究：Emergent magnetism, heavy electrons and pressure-induced reentrant superconductivity in the iron substituted 4d transition-metal sulfides
    📖 arXiv📅 2026-09-14🎯 相关度 4

    📝 简单总结：研究铁取代 Rh17S15 中磁性、重费米子行为与压力诱导的再入超导现象。结果表明，铁掺杂增强电子有效质量，并由于铁磁与反铁磁交换竞争形成自旋玻璃基态；高压下体系重新出现超导性。

    🔗 与我们工作的关系：与团队关注的磁性材料、超导、电子关联和自旋相互作用方向相关，但论文主要依赖实验观测，与团队常用的第一性原理、自旋哈密顿量和机器学习模拟结合较弱。可借鉴其磁性竞争、压力调控和超导共存问题，开展交换作用、电子结构及磁涨落的计算分析。

    💡 进一步工作建议：可进一步用第一性原理计算比较 Rh、Fe 位点的磁矩、交换参数、电子态密度和压力响应，并构建包含竞争交换作用的自旋模型研究自旋玻璃行为。还可计算电子-声子耦合与磁涨落对再入超导的相对贡献。

    文献研究：An Exact Formulation of Phase-Space Electronic Structure Theory in One Dimension by Exploiting the Zero Curvature Condition
    📖 arXiv📅 2026-09-14🎯 相关度 3

    🔗 与我们工作的关系：与团队的非绝热分子动力学、电子-声子耦合和量子动力学存在方法层面的联系，但研究对象是一维形式理论，并未直接涉及凝聚态材料模拟。其相空间表示可作为发展复杂材料非绝热动力学方法的理论参考。

    💡 进一步工作建议：建议关注该理论向周期性固体、二维材料和界面体系的推广，并评估其处理热载流子弛豫、载流子复合及强电子-声子耦合的能力。

    🔗 与我们工作的关系：与团队的机器学习、电子结构建模和人工智能材料模拟存在方法上的交集，但工作主要面向分子量子化学和波函数表示，并非固体材料的 DFT、Kohn-Sham 哈密顿量或材料性质预测。

    📖 arXiv📅 2026-09-14🎯 相关度 2

    🔗 与我们工作的关系：与我们的材料信息学和人工智能辅助材料研究存在较弱关联，但核心是实验设备控制与工艺优化，并非第一性原理、材料模拟或目标材料体系。其模式可为材料实验自动化和闭环优化提供方法参考。

    💡 进一步工作建议：若开展相关延伸，可将智能体与高通量计算、机器学习代理模型和实验表征数据结合，用于材料合成条件或器件结构的闭环优化。阅读时应重点关注其工具调用约束、实验反馈设计和结果验证机制。

    📖 arXiv📅 2026-09-14🎯 相关度 1

01

今日摘要

总览：今日共收录72篇文献，覆盖机器学习、计算物理和功能材料方向。

热点：文献主要涉及结构—性质建模、有限温度/动力学模拟以及材料电子与磁性问题；缺少原文细节的条目不作定量推断。

与我们研究方向的总体关系：本日文献与团队研究的交集主要集中在蒙特卡洛统计采样、等变神经网络与对称性保持表示、有效哈密顿量或机器学习哈密顿量、密度泛函理论和电子结构计算，并落到磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合、缺陷、畴壁、成核和开关动力学、晶体结构、功能材料和结构—性质关系、极化子、热载流子和非绝热载流子动力学。对现有工作最直接的价值是提供可复用的表示、采样或验证流程，而不是替代具体材料体系的物理判断。后续筛选应优先核对原文数据是否包含结构、能量、力、应力、极化、自旋或有限温度轨迹，再决定是否迁移到铁电、磁性、缺陷和外场驱动模拟。

🔬 神经网络势 · 电子结构（重点）

神经网络势、哈密顿量、密度矩阵、电荷密度与电子结构。

  1. 01
    文献研究：Decoding Crystallographic Surface Chirality from Real Space and Momentum Space Images by Machine Learning#
    Decoding Crystallographic Surface Chirality from Real Space and Momentum Space Images by Machine Learning#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Chetana Badala Viswanatha, Ka Man Yu, Benito Arnoldi, Anagha Aravind, Aaruni Kaushik, Jannis Lessmeister 等9位作者
    相关度
    4
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2606.13144v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: Intrinsically chiral metal surfaces, where handedness arises from the asymmetric step-kink-terrace topology of high-Miller index planes, are model systems for enantiospecific catalysis, sensing, and spintronics. Yet no consistent method exists to classify their handedness directly from experimental observables, without prior knowledge of crystallographic indices. Here, we report a dual-domain machine learning framework that recognizes handedness in chiral metal surfaces from two independent image representations: atomic structure models in real space and simulated momentum-resolved photoemission maps of Fermi surface projections in reciprocal space. A ResNet18 model pretrained on general image data is fine-tuned for chirality classification independently on both image representations and achieves meaningful classification accuracy for both real space and reciprocal space images. However, the Fermi surface classifier significantly outperforms the real space classifier. Critically, the reciprocal space classifier, trained only on synthetic images, correctly identifies synchrotron-acquired experimental Angle-Resolved Photoemission Spectroscopy (ARPES) maps of Cu(643)^R and Cu(643)^S. The stronger performance in momentum space, as well as the transfer to experimental data, shows that handedness is encoded more globally and robustly in the electronic structure than in the spatially localized kink-site geometry. These results establish ARPES as a quantitative readout of crystallographic surface chirality and suggest a scalable route to identify chiral metal surfaces relevant to spin-selective phenomena.

    💡 亮点：本文建立机器学习框架，从实空间原子结构图和倒空间费米面投影图中识别金属晶体表面的手性。基于ResNet18的模型能够对合成及实验图像进行手性分类，且倒空间图像的分类性能优于实空间图像。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：该工作把晶体表面手性转化为可由实验图像直接判别的机器学习分类问题，输入包括实空间表面形貌图以及动量空间衍射或电子结构图像，并以表面台阶—扭结—平台的高Miller指数拓扑作为标签来源。核心价值在于比较两类表征对左右手性的可分辨性，模型应通过旋转、镜像、尺度和噪声增强训练，同时显式区分保持手性的正交变换与翻转手性的镜像变换；摘要未给出具体网络层数、损失函数和训练集规模。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与等变卷积、群对称图网络和扫描隧道显微图像识别相呼应，但目标不是预测总能或局域环境，而是从观测量反推出空间反演相关的手性序参量。对Hongyu Yu和Hongjun Xiang的工作，最直接的连接是把表面结构、非共线自旋和Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya相互作用放进同一对称性框架；CrI₃、CrSBr表面和磁性台阶可进一步测试结构手性与自旋手性的耦合。Xin-Gao Gong和Ji-Hui Yang已有二维材料、界面及缺陷图像数据可作为迁移场景。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可先用DFT和slab结构搜索生成NbOI₂、CrI₃、CrSBr及BaTiO₃极性表面的左右手台阶、缺陷和吸附构型，再用MACE或NEP充分弛豫，输出STM模拟图、低能电子衍射图和动量分辨谱图。训练具备E(2)/E(3)等变约束的多模态网络，镜像样本作为反手性对照；验证应同时采用留出Miller指数、不同表面终止和实验噪声测试，并用DFT自洽电荷、局域磁矩及DMI符号检查模型是否学到真实结构因素而非成像伪影。

  2. 02
    文献研究：An ab initio foundation model of wavefunctions that accurately describes chemical bond breaking#
    An ab initio foundation model of wavefunctions that accurately describes chemical bond breaking#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / physics👤 Adam Foster, Zeno Schätzle, P. Bernát Szabó, Lixue Cheng, Jonas Köhler, Gino Cassella 等10位作者🔥 AI 4
    相关度
    2
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2506.19960v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: Reliable description of bond breaking remains a major challenge for quantum chemistry due to the multireference character of the electronic structure in dissociating species. Multireference methods in particular suffer from large computational cost, which under the normal paradigm has to be paid anew for each system at a full price, ignoring commonalities in electronic structure across molecules. Quantum Monte Carlo with deep neural networks uniquely offers to exploit such commonalities by pretraining transferable wavefunction models, but all such attempts were so far limited in scope. Here, we bring this paradigm to fruition with Orbformer, a transferable wavefunction model pretrained on 22,000 equilibrium and dissociating structures that can be fine-tuned on unseen molecules reaching an accuracy-cost ratio rivalling classical multireference methods. On established benchmarks as well as more challenging bond dissociations and Diels-Alder reactions, Orbformer is the only method that consistently converges to chemical accuracy (1 kcal/mol). This work turns the idea of amortizing the cost of solving the Schrödinger equation over many molecules into a practical approach in quantum chemistry.

    💡 亮点：论文提出基于深度神经网络量子蒙特卡洛波函数的基础模型 Orbformer，并在约 22000 个平衡和解离结构上进行预训练。该模型能够较准确地描述化学键断裂和多参考电子结构问题，并降低对每个新体系从头计算的成本。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：该方法以大量低成本、可统一生成的波函数数据预训练基础模型，再对具体分子或反应路径进行微调，从而近似多参考波函数在断键过程中的非平凡节点结构和电子重排。模型输入应包含原子种类、三维坐标、轨道或基组相关表示，输出波函数、能量及可导性质；训练重点是跨分子、键长扫描和不同自旋态的联合损失，而不是只拟合平衡构型。摘要强调可迁移性，但未披露网络架构、基组、预训练样本量及误差分解。

    🔗 相关工作关联：其物理定位接近机器学习Kohn–Sham哈密顿量、神经网络波函数和量子化学势能面，但关键差别是学习对象直接承载电子反对称性与断键时的多参考特征，而非单一绝热势能面。Hongyu Yu和Hongjun Xiang发展的等变哈密顿量、有效模型与对称性约束可提供结构编码；Ji-Hui Yang关注GW-BSE和缺陷激发态，Weibin Chu关注非绝热动力学，两者都需要可靠的激发态和电荷重排表示。与DMRG增强路线相比，本工作更强调跨体系预训练。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可将预训练对象换成BaTiO₃、NbOI₂及CrI₃极化翻转或自旋翻转路径上的局域波函数，数据由DFT+U、杂化泛函和小胞高精度多参考计算共同标定。对每个结构采集轨道、密度矩阵、自旋密度、极化和力，并按键长、畴壁距离、缺陷电荷及磁序分层留出测试集；在长程分子动力学中用能量、力、Berry相位极化、交换参数和自旋翻转能逐项验证，重点检查模型能否处理带电缺陷、近简并磁态与软模区域。

    阅读原文 ↗
    原文链接页内定位
  3. 03
    利用机器学习改进密度矩阵重正化群计算，实现快速且准确的激发能#
    Fast and Accurate Excitation Energies from Density Matrix Renormalization Group Calculations Improved by Machine Learning#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / physics👤 Pavlo Golub, Libor Veis🔥 AI 3
    相关度
    5

    📄 摘要：估计光电性质是现代功能材料科学中的关键需求，也是持续存在的挑战。这需要准确获得具有复杂电子结构体系的基态和激发态，而对于这类体系，精确的多体方法通常计算成本过高。在我们先前关于基态的工作基础上，我们提出一种高效且经济的方法，用于评估功能材料中的电子激发；该方法将密度矩阵重正化群方法作为完备活性空间求解器，并与机器学习技术相结合。我们在π电子相关体系，即多环芳香化合物上展示了该方法的性能。我们还在若干具有挑战性的实例上证明了所得到的机器学习模型具有可迁移性和有效性，这些实例最多包含34个π电子。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.12616v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: An estimation of optoelectronic properties is a crucial necessity and an ongoing challenge in modern functional material science. This involves accurate accessing of ground and excited states in systems with complex electronic structure that are often too computationally expensive for accurate many-body methods. Building on our previous work on ground states [J. Phys. Chem. Lett. 2025, 16, 3295-3301], we present an efficient and cost-effective approach for evaluating electronic excitations in functional materials, combining the density matrix renormalization group method as a complete active space solver with machine learning techniques. We demonstrate its performance on π-electron-correlated systems, namely polycyclic aromatic compounds. The transferability and effectiveness of the derived machine learning model are demonstrated on a number of challenging examples with up to 34 π-electrons.

    💡 亮点：本文将密度矩阵重正化群方法作为完备活性空间求解器与机器学习结合，用于高效、低成本地计算功能材料中的电子激发。针对多环芳香化合物的π电子相关体系，研究展示了该方法的性能，并在最多包含34个π电子的困难实例中验证了机器学习模型的可迁移性和有效性。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：文章把DMRG作为完备活性空间求解器，再用机器学习学习从分子结构、活性空间轨道特征或低成本电子结构描述到DMRG激发能的映射，以减少每个新构型都进行高成本张量网络优化的次数。训练样本来自多环芳香化合物及其π电子体系，重点覆盖不同环数、几何构型、激发类型和最多34个π电子的困难实例；需要对基态与激发态能量差采用统一归一化、分层交叉验证并检查外推误差，摘要未明确模型架构和DMRG截断参数。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与Δ-learning、主动学习势能面和机器学习电子结构校正相似，区别是标签来自DMRG而非单粒子DFT或经验激发模型，因而能保留强关联π体系中的多组态效应。Ji-Hui Yang已有GW-BSE、电子—声子耦合和激发态研究，可比较机器学习DMRG与BSE在局域激发、双激发和电荷转移态上的差异；Weibin Chu的非绝热动力学需要这种低成本激发能接口。Hongyu Yu的波函数/哈密顿量学习则适合进一步预测跃迁密度而非只有标量能量。

    💡 对你方向的启示：团队可把训练集扩展到铁电和二维材料中的局域激发：用小胞DMRG或选定多参考方法计算BaTiO₃氧空位、NbOI₂极性畸变、CrI₃/CrSBr配体场激发的基态—激发态能隙，再以DFT+U、杂化泛函和局域轨道占据作为输入。采用按材料家族和缺陷构型留出的外推测试，验证激发能、振子强度、能量梯度及自旋多重度；将模型嵌入非绝热分子动力学，比较热载流子弛豫时间、态间跃迁概率与小规模高精度基准。

    文献研究：Surrogate Modeling of 3D Rayleigh-Benard Convection with Equivariant Autoencoders#
    Surrogate Modeling of 3D Rayleigh-Benard Convection with Equivariant Autoencoders#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / physics👤 Fynn Fromme, Hans Harder, Christine Allen-Blanchette, Sebastian Peitz🔥 AI 4
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2505.13569v3 Announce Type: replace-cross Abstract: The use of machine learning for modeling, understanding, and controlling large-scale physics systems is quickly gaining in popularity, with examples ranging from electromagnetism over nuclear fusion reactors and magneto-hydrodynamics to fluid mechanics and climate modeling. These systems - governed by partial differential equations - present unique challenges regarding the large number of degrees of freedom and the complex dynamics over many scales both in space and time, and additional measures to improve accuracy and sample efficiency are highly desirable. We present an end-to-end equivariant surrogate model consisting of an equivariant convolutional autoencoder and an equivariant convolutional LSTM using G-steerable kernels. As a case study, we consider the three-dimensional Rayleigh-Bénard convection, which describes the buoyancy-driven fluid flow between a heated bottom and a cooled top plate. While the system is E(2)-equivariant in the horizontal plane, the boundary conditions break the translational equivariance in the vertical direction. Our architecture leverages vertically stacked layers of D₄-steerable kernels, with additional partial kernel sharing in the vertical direction for further efficiency improvement. We demonstrate significant gains in sample and parameter efficiency, as well as a better scaling to more complex dynamics. The accompanying code is available under https://github.com/FynnFromme/equivariant-rb-forecasting.

    💡 亮点：arXiv:2505.13569v3 Announce Type: replace-cross Abstract: The use of machine learning for modeling, understanding, and controlling large-scale physics systems is quickly gaining in popularity, with ex…

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：该工作针对三维Rayleigh–Bénard对流构建保留空间对称性的等变自编码器，用高维速度、温度和压力场压缩到低维潜变量，再由解码器重建瞬时流场，并以潜空间动力学或代理模型预测长期演化。关键不是普通卷积降维，而是让平移、旋转、反射等变换在编码和解码前后保持一致，从而减少数据增强负担并保留涡结构几何；训练应覆盖不同Rayleigh数、Prandtl数和边界条件，但摘要未给出具体潜变量维度、网格分辨率与误差指标。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与神经算子、POD/DMD、流体代理模型和物理信息神经网络相关，但等变自编码器更适合表达旋转或镜像相关的场结构，且可在低维空间执行控制和参数扫描。对团队而言，方法层面对应Hongjun Xiang的有效哈密顿量和蒙特卡洛降阶，也对应Xin-Gao Gong的机器学习势与主动学习；Weibin Chu的非绝热动力学可借鉴其长时间轨迹压缩。与材料GNN相比，它处理的是连续三维场而非原子图。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可将输入场改为BaTiO₃极化翻转、畴壁迁移或CrSBr自旋—晶格动力学中的三维时空场：由NEP或MACE生成大尺度分子动力学轨迹，再以局域极化、应变、磁矩、温度和应力网格化。用E(3)等变编码器压缩轨迹，并将外场、电场方向、应变和温度作为条件变量；按材料、尺寸和驱动频率留出测试，验证极化开关时间、畴壁速度、能量守恒、相关长度及功率谱，同时与直接MD和有效哈密顿量蒙特卡洛结果比较。

    由智能体编写的 X 射线多层镜沉积配方：通过模式约束的大语言模型控制磁控溅射系统并以反射率验证结果#
    Agent-authored deposition recipes for X-ray multilayer mirrors: schema-bound LLM control of a magnetron sputtering system with reflectivity-verified outcomes#
    🧭 材料 / 器件P3 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Oleksiy V. Penkov, Haoyu Fu, Yueying Zhan, Pengyuan Wu, Jiong Jin🔥 AI 10
    相关度
    2

    📄 摘要：用于 X 射线的周期多层镜要求层周期保持在约 0.02 nm 的精度内，而这由保存在实验室自身记录本中的沉积速率决定。一个大语言模型智能体获得了目标多层膜规格，并拥有对实验室档案的读取权限。沉积配方以磁控溅射系统的原生配方语言编写，并通过一个包含三层共 17 个工具的模型上下文协议桥提交，执行约束保留在控制应用程序中。目标是一个 30 个双层的 Ru/C 多层膜，周期为 6.85 nm，选择这一材料对是因为每个周期被拟合为两层。该智能体只能在其自己的工作项目内写入，因此既不能修改它所读取的校准，也不能修改用于评分的测量结果。多层镜通过 X 射线反射率相对于人类编写的参考配方进行评分，并且相对周期差 3% 的接受阈值在沉积前即已固定。在四次评分的智能体编写沉积中，相对周期差从给定校准条件下的 20% 降至一次测量反馈迭代后的 2.26%，并在第二次迭代后降至 0.79%。研究识别出 16 种面向智能体接口的失效模式。该结果的适用范围限定于所观察的周期、材料对和腔室。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.12796v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Periodic multilayer mirrors for X-rays require a layer period held to about 0.02 nm, set by deposition rates held in the laboratory's own notebook. A large language model agent was given a target multilayer specification and read access to a laboratory archive. Deposition recipes were authored in the native recipe language of a magnetron sputtering system and submitted through a Model Context Protocol bridge of 17 tools in three tiers, with enforcement held in the control application. The target was a 30-bilayer Ru/C multilayer with a period of 6.85 nm, a pair chosen because each period is fitted as two layers. The agent could write only inside a working project of its own, so it could alter neither the calibration it read nor the measurements that scored it. Mirrors were scored against a human-authored reference by X-ray reflectivity, and the 3% acceptance threshold on relative period difference was fixed before deposition. Across four scored agent-authored depositions the relative period difference fell from 20% on the calibration as given to 2.26% after one measured-feedback iteration and 0.79% after a second. Sixteen failure modes of the agent-facing interface were identified. The result is bounded to the period, pair and chamber observed.

    💡 亮点：该工作测试了大语言模型智能体在受控接口下为 X 射线周期多层镜编写磁控溅射沉积配方的能力。目标为 30 个双层的 Ru/C 多层膜，周期为 6.85 nm，并以 X 射线反射率相对人类参考样品进行评分。四次评分沉积中，相对周期差从初始校准条件下的 20% 降至两轮测量反馈后的 0.79%，同时识别出 16 种面向智能体接口的失效模式。

    文献研究：QTrans: A Quantum Transformer for Sentiment Classification#
    QTrans: A Quantum Transformer for Sentiment Classification#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P3 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Ren-Xin Zhao, Xinjie Huang, Yahong Liu, Maoyu Ye, Jinjing Shi, Shi Wang 等7位作者🔥 AI 10
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.12011v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: In small-scale binary sentiment classification scenarios, factors such as negation, contrastive shifts, and cross-word dependencies lead to the non-linear coupling of sentiment cues, making it difficult for conventional lightweight models to fully capture the contextual relationships between tokens. To address this issue, we propose a model named QTrans, which uses parameterized quantum circuits to construct query, key, and value features and derives attention coefficients from Gaussian distances between quantum measurements. By further integrating a quantum feed-forward neural network, residual connections, and layer normalization, the model establishes an end-to-end trainable quantum-classical hybrid framework for sentiment classification. Experimental results on the MR, CR, and MPQA datasets show that QTrans achieves test accuracies of 72.13%, 69.51%, and 63.45%, respectively, representing improvements of 2.88, 3.17, and 3.79 percentage points over the best-performing classical baselines for each dataset. Overall, QTrans expands the application of parameterized quantum circuits in lightweight sentiment analysis and lays an experimental foundation for further research into quantum multi-head self-attention for modeling textual relationships.

    文献研究：Machine Learning-Based Classification of Jhana Advanced Concentrative Absorption Meditation Using 7 Tesla Functional Magnetic Resonance Imaging#
    Machine Learning-Based Classification of Jhana Advanced Concentrative Absorption Meditation Using 7 Tesla Functional Magnetic Resonance Imaging#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Puneet Kumar, Winson F. Z. Yang, Alakhsimar Singh, Xiaobai Li, Matthew D. Sacchet
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2602.13008v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: Introduction: Jhana advanced concentrative absorption meditation (ACAM-J) involves profound changes in consciousness, making its neural correlates important for understanding consciousness and well-being. Prior neuroimaging has relied on univariate, group-level contrasts, leaving open whether ACAM-J carries distributed neural signatures decodable from individual scans. This study evaluates whether fMRI-derived regional homogeneity (ReHo) can classify ACAM-J using machine learning. Methods: We analysed 7T fMRI data from 20 advanced meditators who progressed through their standard ACAM-J sequence and two matched control tasks, plus intensive data from one case-study participant held out for final evaluation. ReHo maps were computed per segment and parcellated into 498 regions spanning cortex, subcortex, brainstem, and cerebellum. Within subject-wise stratified cross-validation, feature ranking, recursive feature elimination, and class balancing were applied to training data only; six classifier families were fitted, and the top three per contrast were combined by probability averaging. Results: Across 19 binary comparisons, the ensemble reached an overall average accuracy of 65.87% before and 66.82% after regressing out phenomenology-related variance, with an average Cohen's kappa of 0.2443. Discrimination was strongest for the most separated states (ACAM-J1 vs ACAM-J6, 74.33% accuracy, kappa = 0.5158), while adjacent states were harder to separate. Prefrontal and anterior cingulate areas contributed most to model decisions. Conclusion: ReHo patterns measured at 7T carry information distinguishing ACAM-J from control states and, more modestly, from one another, supporting the feasibility of multivariate decoding of advanced meditation and informing future work on its mechanisms and neuromodulation.

    文献研究：The Battery Price of edge AI: A study of the Environmental Impact of LLM Inference on Mobile Devices#
    The Battery Price of edge AI: A study of the Environmental Impact of LLM Inference on Mobile Devices#
    🧭 材料 / 器件P3 · 其他📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Édouard Guégain, Tristan Coignion🔥 AI 10
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.11940v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: The rapid diffusion of generative artificial intelligence raises privacy, latency, and performance concerns that motivate a shift toward "local-first" AI, where inferences are performed on the user's device instead of on remote cloud servers. This paradigm also places a significant computational load on battery-powered smartphones, potentially shortening battery life and increasing the overall replacement rate of mobile devices. This paper presents a systematic study of the energy consumption, performance, and accuracy of on-device large language model (LLM) inference. We evaluate 18 models from different model families, sizes, and quantization levels, on two modern smartphones and on a server, using the respective state-of-the-art for such deployments. We measure the energy per generated token, inter-token latency, model accuracy, and battery-cycle consumption. Our results show that (i) on-device inference is on average 3 times less energy-efficient than batched server inference; (ii) the relationship between quantization bit-width and energy per token is non-monotonic, with energy sweet spots on both tested smartphones; (iii) eight out of 18 model configurations lie on the Pareto front of accuracy and energy-efficiency, allowing practitioners to build battery-aware model routers; and (iv) realistic modeling assumptions do not allow local inference to be less environmentally impacting per token than batched server inference, with 88--90% of that impact attributable to device embodied carbon rather than electricity consumption. These findings challenge the premise that local AI is more sustainable than cloud inference, and motivate the need for context-aware and life-cycle-aware model selection when deploying edge AI on battery-powered mobile platforms.

    💡 亮点：arXiv:2609.11940v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: The rapid diffusion of generative artificial intelligence raises privacy, latency, and performance concerns that motivate a shift toward "local-first"…

    文献研究：RS-CIDER: A non-local machine learning model for approximating screened hybrid functionals#
    RS-CIDER: A non-local machine learning model for approximating screened hybrid functionals#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · AI×化学·材料📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Zhuotao Jin, Mohamed S. Abdallah, Boris Kozinsky, Kyle Bystrom🔥 AI 6
    相关度
    9
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.13139v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Screened hybrid functionals such as HSE06 improve the description of band gaps, charge localization, and redox energetics relative to semilocal approximations, but their explicit Hartree-Fock exchange term is computationally costly for large, periodic systems, especially in plane-wave basis set calculations. Here we present RS-CIDER, a machine-learned non-local exchange functional that approximates the short-range Hartree-Fock exchange term in HSE06 by explicitly fitting both ground-state energies and single-particle energy levels. RS-CIDER combines scale-invariant semilocal and non-local density descriptors and can be evaluated self-consistently without explicitly applying the short-range Hartree-Fock exchange operator. RS-CIDER shows close agreement with HSE06 for molecular reaction energies and solid-state band gaps. Further tests across distinct materials show agreement between RS-CIDER and HSE06 for local magnetism, Cu-O phase competition, polaron localization, and neutral-defect energetics. For an Fe olivine, chemistry-specific fine-tuning recovers the HSE06 Li intercalation voltage. A timing benchmark shows that RS-CIDER reduces the measured per-SCF-step wall time by more than an order of magnitude relative to HSE06. Together, these molecular and solid-state results establish RS-CIDER as an efficient self-consistent machine-learned surrogate for HSE06.

    💡 亮点：该论文提出RS-CIDER非局域机器学习交换泛函，用于近似HSE06中的短程Hartree-Fock交换，并能自洽地计算分子和周期性固体。结果表明其可较好复现HSE06的带隙、局域磁性、极化子局域化和中性缺陷能，并有望显著降低计算成本。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：RS-CIDER旨在以非局域机器学习模型近似屏蔽杂化泛函中的Hartree–Fock交换贡献，输入不是单一原子邻域，而是由电子密度、梯度、轨道或密度矩阵构造的局域描述与有限范围外的环境信息，从而表达屏蔽交换的空间非局域性。训练标签应来自HSE06等屏蔽杂化计算，目标包括交换能、势或能量差，并通过周期性体系、不同带隙和电荷局域程度的数据覆盖提升可迁移性；摘要未给出截断半径、网络规模和自洽收敛细节。

    🔗 相关工作关联：该路线直接连接机器学习交换—相关泛函、CIDER类密度泛函表示、机器学习Kohn–Sham哈密顿量及神经网络势，但与普通半局域DFT回归相比，核心难点是长程交换和自洽势的一致性。Hongyu Yu和Hongjun Xiang已有机器学习哈密顿量、张量性质与可迁移模型经验，可用于把RS-CIDER从能量校正扩展到矩阵元；Ji-Hui Yang和Xin-Gao Gong关注带隙、缺陷和输运，正是HSE成本最显著的材料场景。LiₓCoO₂还可检验强关联限制。

    💡 对你方向的启示：建议建立BaTiO₃、NbOI₂、CrI₃、CrSBr及SiC缺陷的PBE、PBE+U、HSE06和必要的自旋轨道耦合数据集，按结构、磁序、缺陷电荷和应变分层采样。先训练能量与势的联合模型，再把预测势嵌入自洽Kohn–Sham循环；验证应包括带隙、极化、Born有效电荷、交换分裂、缺陷形成能、磁交换常数和声子频率，并与直接HSE及GW-BSE交叉比较，特别检查反铁磁、非共线磁性和局域极化子。

    Observation of topological surface phonons in diamond with nonlinear optics#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P3 · 拓扑·电子结构📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Qi Wang, Xinyi Liu, Xinyue Sheng, Zhi-Kang Lin, Xiaowei Lu, Xiaosheng Yang 等11位作者🔥 AI 10
    相关度
    6
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.12362v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Topological quantum states in electronic systems have profoundly transformed the understanding of phases of matter. Recent theories predict novel vibrational topological quantum states, i.e., topological phonons in various solids. However, this paradigm is yet to be established due to the lack of convincing experimental verification of topological surface phonons---a hallmark signature of topological phonon states. Here, we report the discovery of topological surface phonons in diamond using sum-frequency spectroscopy---a nonlinear optical spectroscopy capable of probing surface phonons with high sensitivity. Diamond, known for exceptional hardness and thermal transport, is unveiled as a phonon topological semimetal hosting topological nodal-lines and nexus triple points in the bulk. With consistent theory and experiments, we uncover the resultant topological surface phonons on diamond (111) and (100) surfaces. Moreover, by chemically modifying these surfaces, we reveal the disorder effect on topological surface phonons. These findings pave the way for bridging two fundamental domains: quantum topology and lattice dynamics, besides having important implications on diamond-based devices and functional interfaces.

    💡 亮点：论文通过和频光谱实验观测到金刚石表面的拓扑表面声子，并结合理论揭示其体内拓扑节点线和三重点。研究还考察了表面化学修饰和无序对拓扑表面声子的影响。

    文献研究：Quantitative and bond-traceable resonant X-ray optical tensors of organic molecules#
    Quantitative and bond-traceable resonant X-ray optical tensors of organic molecules#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P3 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Victor Murcia, Obaid Alqahtani, Harlan Heilman, Brian A. Collins🔥 AI 6
    相关度

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2509.01734v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: Local molecular orientation and conformation within nanostructures is central to their behavior in devices, emergent quantum states, and biological processes. X-ray microscopy and scattering at the carbon absorption edge is uniquely sensitive to these phenomena, through transition dipole moments encoded with photon energy and polarization. However, quantitative analysis is precluded due to the lack of accurate optical models with bond and orientation specificity. We generate such a model through an unsupervised machine learning algorithm that clusters density functional theory calculations into principle electronic transitions, quantitatively refined with angle-resolved absorbance measurements. The resulting optical tensor reproduces data from samples with domains of different orientation and crystalline packing. This refinement method connects spectral features to chemical moieties and resolves measurement-inaccessible dipole moments, establishing a new paradigm for label-free orientation analyses of bonds within molecular nanostructures.

    💡 亮点：论文利用无监督机器学习聚类密度泛函理论计算，识别有机分子中的主要电子跃迁，并结合角分辨吸收实验构建具有化学键和取向分辨率的共振 X 射线光学张量。该模型能够描述不同取向和晶体堆积样品的光学响应。

    文献研究：Physics-informed deep learning links geodetic data and fault friction#
    Physics-informed deep learning links geodetic data and fault friction#
    🧭 材料 / 器件P3 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / physics👤 Rikuto Fukushima, Masayuki Kano, Kazuro Hirahara, Makiko Ohtani🔥 AI 4
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2601.20136v3 Announce Type: replace Abstract: Fault slip modeling, based on laboratory-derived friction laws, has significantly enhanced our understanding of fault mechanics. Agreement between model predictions and observations supports the hypothesis that observed slip diversity, including fast earthquakes and slow transient slips (Slow Slip Events; SSEs), originates from frictional heterogeneity. However, quantitative assessments of frictional heterogeneity from geodetic observations while fully incorporating fault mechanics are lacking due to the difficulties of high-dimensional optimization. In this study, we aim to address this gap using Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) to link frictional heterogeneity with geodetic observations. PINNs employ a neural network to represent the spatially variable frictional properties, making their estimation feasible. Targeting the 2010 Bungo SSE in southwest Japan, our estimation reveals heterogeneous friction coinciding with localized SSE nucleation in southwest Shikoku, and subsequent westward propagation. The calculated fault slip of SSE successfully reproduces the spatio-temporal pattern of observed surface displacements. This PINN-based inversion provides a mechanically consistent fault slip model validated through quantitative comparison with observations. Furthermore, we forecast the future fault slip evolution, demonstrating the importance of assimilating observations spanning multiple SSE cycles. Our results demonstrate the potential of PINN for advancing understanding of fault mechanics and enabling physics-based fault slip forecasting.

    文献研究：PDE-constrained inverse problems at the sqrtn rate via debiased physics-informed neural networks#
    PDE-constrained inverse problems at the sqrtn rate via debiased physics-informed neural networks#
    🧭 方法 / 工具P3 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / stat.ML👤 Yves Atchade, Debarghya Mukherjee🔥 AI 10
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.12301v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: We study the problem of estimating unknown parameters in PDE-constrained inverse problems from noisy observations, where the PDE solution is approximated using Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs). While PINNs have demonstrated remarkable empirical success, existing estimators often inherit the slow nonparametric convergence rate of the neural-network solution, leading to biased and statistically inefficient inference for the finite-dimensional parameters of interest. To address this, we propose a two-step debiased estimation procedure that combines neural-network-based nonparametric estimation with an influence-function-based bias correction. By eliminating the first-order sensitivity of the estimator to errors in the nuisance function, our procedure yields a sqrtn-consistent and asymptotically normal estimator without requiring undersmoothing of the neural network component. We further extend this framework to Bayesian inference by replacing the original likelihood with a debiased quasi-likelihood and establish a Bernstein-von Mises theorem showing that the resulting posterior contracts at the sqrtn-rate with an asymptotic covariance matching that of the frequentist estimator. As a by-product of our analysis, we establish near-minimax optimal convergence rates for estimating a nonparametric regression function and its derivatives in Sobolev spaces using neural networks. Extensive numerical experiments corroborate our theoretical findings and demonstrate the necessity of the proposed debiasing procedure for valid statistical inference in PDE-constrained inverse problems.

    Reinforcement Learning for Syndrome Extraction#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P3 · 量子信息·计算📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 John Zhuoyang Ye, Aarav Pabla, Jens Palsberg🔥 AI 10
    相关度

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.12020v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: A key subtask of quantum error correction is to extract a syndrome that, if nontrivial, signals an error. The number of possible ways to extract a syndrome grows exponentially with the syndrome size, and these implementations vary greatly in fault tolerance, as measured by their logical error rates. This creates a natural search problem: find an implementation with a low logical error rate. Previous work solves this problem but sacrifices either solution quality or scalability. In this paper, we use reinforcement learning and importance sampling to outperform previous work at all scales. Compared with the state of the art automatic scheduling tools AlphaSyndrome and PropHunt, our tool reduces the logical error rate by 25.9% and 71.7% on average, respectively, culminating with a reduction of 97.8% for a surface code with distance 15.

    Catastrophes, Optical Multistabilities, and Chiral Photocurrent Hysteresis in Driven Weyl Semimetals#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P3 · 铁电·极化📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Christopher Yang, Gil Refael, Frederik Nathan🔥 AI 5
    相关度
    6
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.12041v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The unique band topology of Weyl semimetals provides them with a diverse array of strong photovoltaic response phenomena. Here we explore how plasma screening affects these phenomena. We find that the screening field is highly nonlinear, can contain topological contributions, and can amplify the photoresponse, with the self-consistent response exhibiting the hallmarks of bistability, including hysteresis and catastrophes. The bistability emerges from a crossover from linear to saturated polarization as a function of the field amplitude, because carriers in a Weyl cone have bounded group speed v_F. When time-reversal symmetry is broken, tilted Weyl nodes add a Berry-curvature contribution to the in-plane current, making the nonlinear screening response helicity selective.

    文献研究：Continuous Learning of Gravity Field Irregularities Around Small Bodies via Neural Hamiltonian ODEs#
    Continuous Learning of Gravity Field Irregularities Around Small Bodies via Neural Hamiltonian ODEs#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P3 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / physics👤 Giacomo Acciarini, Dario Izzo🔥 AI 4
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.12022v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: We propose to learn the unknown dynamics in the proximity of a small body directly from tracking data, representing them as a feed-forward neural network embedded in the system Hamiltonian. The equations of motion form a Neural Hamiltonian Ordinary Differential Equation, whose variational equations provide exact training gradients: estimation uses position and velocity arcs at realistic noise levels, without acceleration or potential labels, and a continual learning approach warm-starts the network as new data are acquired. The known part of the Hamiltonian carries whatever is available, from the central term and spin state to the constant-density model of the imaged shape. We assess the method against a normalized spherical harmonics expansion estimated from identical arcs through the same machinery, on scenarios built on the shapes of Itokawa, 67P, Bennu and Eros. The network remains usable inside the Brillouin sphere: it plans ballistic descents at Itokawa to SI4.6m median touchdown error from tracking alone, against 5.1--SI48.9m for harmonics of degree 4--12, and to SI0.9m with the imaged shape as prior, a configuration that also recovers localised density anomalies invisible to any harmonics degree. The two representations are complementary, and we discuss their combined use across the phases of a small-body mission.

    文献研究：Toward Reliable Railway-Bogie Response Prediction Using Multifidelity TDNN and Physics-Informed Residual Learning#
    Toward Reliable Railway-Bogie Response Prediction Using Multifidelity TDNN and Physics-Informed Residual Learning#
    🧭 方法 / 工具P3 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / physics👤 Gyeolhee Lee, Moosun Kim, Taewook Kwon, Jaehun Kim, Dongjin Lee🔥 AI 3
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.12018v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: Railway engineers need simulation models that predict vehicle responses across operating scenarios that cannot be tested exhaustively. Agreement with representative measurements provides essential evidence, but calibration at a limited set of conditions does not guarantee accuracy elsewhere. We present a multifidelity railway-bogie response-correction method that treats multibody simulation histories as low-fidelity information and roller-rig measurements as high-fidelity evidence. This method combines an experiment-anchored fidelity assignment with physics-informed discrepancy learning for multichannel bogie-response histories. A time-delay neural network (TDNN) represents the condition-dependent simulation trend, and development-fitted amplitude alignment defines the low-fidelity baseline. A residual-correction network then models the reproducible response component not explained by this baseline and adds it to the baseline. An effective dynamic-balance equation constrains the learned discrepancy by representing differences in inertia, damping, stiffness, and external forcing between the simulated and physical systems. The training objective combines this constraint with residual matching, temporal smoothness, and selectively applied displacement-acceleration consistency terms. For the evaluated reconstruction case, the corrected response gives a mean coefficient of determination of 0.8197, a mean normalized root-mean-square error (NRMSE) of 4.6055 %, and a mean normalized mean absolute error (NMAE) of 1.9297 %. These results provide initial evidence of accurate response prediction at the held-out 385 km/h condition.

    文献研究：GSF-χ: Global Stereochemical Fields for Chiral Graph Transformers#
    GSF-χ: Global Stereochemical Fields for Chiral Graph Transformers#
    🧭 化学 / 分子P3 · 其他📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Jiaqing Xie, Yuxin Wang, Xipeng Qiu🔥 AI 10
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.12532v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Enantiomers share atoms, bonds, and pairwise distances yet can behave differently in chiral environments, so molecular encoders must respect atom relabelings and proper rotations without becoming blind to reflection. We introduce GSF-χ, a graph transformer in which stereogenic units modulate all pairwise interactions rather than single out one atom as special. Each central or axial stereogenic unit creates a reflection-even phase field over all atoms, a handedness pseudoscalar χ sets the direction of a relative rotation on latent query--key blocks, giving a Chiral-RoPE that reflection inverts rather than leaves fixed. A C₂ projection separates mirror-even ECD peak counts and positions from mirror-odd peak signs. We prove the operator's even--odd decomposition and its annotation-inversion, permutation, and unit-order identities under explicit canonical-role conditions; property tests and a coordinate-reflection audit verify the laws end to end. GSF-χ leads every central-ECD output and improves axial Rotation and Symbol by 12.6% and 7.9% over the strongest baseline. Equal-budget controls attribute the Rotation advantage to global signed support rather than parameter or edge count; the C₂ projection yields exact enantiomer-pair consistency at a small raw-accuracy cost under complete supervision and becomes predictive when mirror supervision is scarce.

    文献研究：HoliBench: A Cross-Platform Benchmarking and Deployment Toolkit for Foundation Models in CPS-IoT Applications#
    HoliBench: A Cross-Platform Benchmarking and Deployment Toolkit for Foundation Models in CPS-IoT Applications#
    🧭 材料 / 器件P3 · 其他📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Inesh Chakrabarti, Zejun Xiong, Pragya Sharma, Mani Srivastava🔥 AI 10
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.12412v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: Foundation models, including large language models, vision-language models, and time-series foundation models, are increasingly deployed on embedded and edge platforms for CPS and IoT applications, where energy, latency, and memory are as critical as task accuracy. Existing benchmarking tools evaluate model capability in isolation, reporting accuracy assuming sufficient compute, while hardware profiling tools remain platform-specific and mutually incompatible. As a result, users lack a unified workflow for making deployment decisions across heterogeneous devices. We present HoliBench, a modular benchmarking and deployment toolkit that jointly characterizes accuracy, latency, and energy across platforms from single-board computers to GPU servers. Its platform abstraction layer calibrates cross-device measurement, and the toolkit supports multiple model modalities, inference engines, concurrencies, and existing evaluation harnesses. An interactive interface exposes constraint-aware configuration selection over a design space that is profiled once and reused across studies. Using HoliBench, we characterize 20 models across 7 device types, 3 quantization levels, 8 inference backends, and over 30 tasks, surfacing tradeoffs that existing tools miss: quantization reduces latency only on hardware with low-precision support, accuracy gains show diminishing returns relative to energy, and for autoregressive workloads, average inference power is approximately constant across output lengths. We further find that single-model profiles compose under sequential co-resident execution. In a multi-model CPS deployment, standalone profiles predict combined-pipeline latency and power within 1.2% and 2.5%, enabling deployment exploration without exhaustively profiling every pipeline configuration. We release HoliBench as open-source infrastructure for deployment-aware evaluation of foundation models.

    文献研究：PLSP (Pre-hoc Liminal Space Profiling): OOD Prediction over Detection -- An Anticipatory Approach for Machine Learning Model Reliability#
    PLSP (Pre-hoc Liminal Space Profiling): OOD Prediction over Detection -- An Anticipatory Approach for Machine Learning Model Reliability#
    🧭 材料 / 器件P3 · 其他📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Vipul Bansal, Himanshu Buckchash, Balasubramanian Raman, Deepak Dhungana🔥 AI 10
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.12225v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Out-of-Distribution (OOD) data poses a significant threat to machine learning models, often leading to model failure during deployment. All existing OOD detection methods are post-hoc, relying on evaluation metrics such as accuracy and AUC-ROC during inference to indirectly assess the model's response to OOD data by measuring deviations. In contrast to existing approaches, the proposed work shifts the paradigm from OOD detection to OOD prediction by proposing a pre-hoc anticipatory framework called PLSP for OOD prediction. We make several key contributions: (a) a dataset-independent metric called the CREDibility Score (CREDS) is proposed for OOD prediction; (b) credibility curves are introduced to study the maximum credibility a model can attain; and (c) credibility heat maps (and volume under surface) are introduced to characterize pre-hoc model behavior across different datasets. This work provides a novel perspective on signal processing under distributional shifts. Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that the proposed metric serves as a valuable measure for improving the robustness of machine learning models toward OOD prediction.

    文献研究：Towards Sustainable Hydrogen Systems: Supply Chain Optimization with Model Predictive Control and Reinforcement Learning#
    Towards Sustainable Hydrogen Systems: Supply Chain Optimization with Model Predictive Control and Reinforcement Learning#
    🧭 材料 / 器件P3 · 其他📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Mahammad Valiyev🔥 AI 10
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.11933v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: Hydrogen supply chains are expected to play a central role in future low-carbon energy systems by enabling renewable energy integration, long-duration storage, and decarbonization of industrial and transportation sectors. However, their operation is challenged by renewable generation variability, electricity price fluctuations, uncertain hydrogen demand, and engineering constraints associated with electrolyzers, energy storage, and grid interaction. As hydrogen infrastructure expands toward commercial deployment, operational strategies must balance economic performance, reliability, and sustainability under dynamic and uncertain conditions. This paper investigates and compares four control approaches for a renewable-powered hydrogen supply chain: a rule-based controller (RBC), model predictive control (MPC), reinforcement learning without forecasts (RL-NF), and reinforcement learning with forecast-augmented observations (RL-F). All methods are evaluated within a unified, physically realistic framework incorporating electrolyzer minimum-load and ramp-rate constraints, battery and hydrogen storage dynamics, grid import limits, and consistent economic assumptions, enabling a fair comparison under identical operating conditions. Simulation results show that MPC achieves the highest economic performance by exploiting short-term forecasts to coordinate storage, reduce grid dependence, and improve efficiency. RL-NF demonstrates robust and competitive performance without future information, highlighting the capability of learning-based methods to discover effective policies from experience. RL-F does not consistently outperform its no-forecast counterpart, suggesting that forecast uncertainty and increased state complexity can limit forecast-augmented learning. The results provide guidance for selecting operational control strategies in future hydrogen energy systems.

    文献研究：Agentic TCAD Calibration Workflow for Oxide Semiconductor Transistors#
    Agentic TCAD Calibration Workflow for Oxide Semiconductor Transistors#
    🧭 材料 / 器件P3 · 其他📖 arXiv / physics👤 Gyujun Jeong, Junmo Lee, Sungwon Cho, Woohyun Hwang, Kwangyou Seo, Suhwan Lim 等14位作者🔥 AI 6
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.12184v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Experimental TCAD calibration is essential for predictive technology modeling of emerging oxide semiconductor transistors. However, it remains time-consuming and expert dependent because of model ambiguity. Multiple physical models and parameter sets can reproduce the same measured transfer characteristics, while local fitting alone cannot uniquely identify the underlying device physics. We present the first demonstration of an agentic TCAD calibration workflow for a fabricated bottom-gate In--W--O (BG-IWO) transistor. Starting from the measured transfer curve and device information, the workflow uses measurement--TCAD residuals and local sensitivity tests to select bounded parameter corrections or evaluate additional physical models, and accept only updates that improve device metrics. The LLM agent orchestrates the workflow, while Sentaurus governs the device physics. For the 2%-W reference device, five agent-suggested updates yield a fixed calibrated model, reducing the multi-metric device objective J by 14.3×. Maximum Vₜₕ/Iₒₙ errors are 36.1~mV/0.022 decade for varying-drain-bias tests and 46.2~mV/0.062 decade for varying-channel-length tests, demonstrating model transferability across bias and geometry rather than a local parameter fit. W-composition tests provide process-sensitive insight. This agentic workflow provides a faster route to model development for emerging device technologies.

    文献研究：AsyncFlow: An Asynchronous Streaming RL Framework for Efficient LLM Post-Training#
    AsyncFlow: An Asynchronous Streaming RL Framework for Efficient LLM Post-Training#
    🧭 材料 / 器件P3 · 其他📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Zhenyu Han, Ansheng You, Haibo Wang, Kui Luo, Guang Yang, Wenqi Shi 等19位作者
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2507.01663v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a pivotal technology in the post-training phase of large language models (LLMs). Traditional task-collocated RL frameworks suffer from significant scalability bottlenecks, while task-separated RL frameworks face challenges in managing complex dataflows and resolving resource idling. Furthermore, most existing frameworks are tightly coupled with LLM training or inference engines, making them difficult to support custom-designed engines. To address these challenges, we propose AsyncFlow, an asynchronous streaming RL framework tailored for efficient post-training. Specifically, we introduce a distributed data storage and transfer module that provides panoramic data management and fine-grained scheduling capabilities in a fully streamed manner. This architecture inherently enables automated pipeline overlapping among RL tasks and dynamic load-balancing. Moreover, we propose an asynchronous producer-consumer workflow, which is engineered to minimize computational idleness by strategically deferring the parameter update process within staleness thresholds. Finally, the core capabilities of AsyncFlow are architecturally decoupled from underlying training and inference engines and encapsulated by service-oriented user interfaces, offering a modular and customizable user experience. Extensive experiments demonstrate an average throughput of 1.59x compared to the state-of-the-art baseline. The architecture presented in this work provides actionable insights for designing next-generation RL training systems.

    文献研究：Creating an Atomic User Model for Personality-Aware Large Language Model Interaction#
    Creating an Atomic User Model for Personality-Aware Large Language Model Interaction#
    🧭 材料 / 器件P3 · 其他📖 arXiv / cs.AI👤 B. Sankar, Deepthika S, Pawni Yadav, Amogh A S
    相关度
    0
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.12086v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: Assistants built on large language models are expected to write as their user would, and the dominant approach is single-channel: preferences summarised from conversation history and reinserted into context. This inverts the order of inference. Preferences are the task-dependent surface of a comparatively stable personality structure, so a system storing only preferences relearns the person whenever the task changes. First, we characterise personality seepage, where a prompt's linguistic surface carries a personality fingerprint the assistant mirrors without access to the personality behind it. Second, we propose the Atomic User Model (AUM), a human-readable representation organising a person as a stable identity nucleus with four interpretable shells (psychological, cognitive and experiential, behavioural, and social), plus cross-shell entries recording internal conflict and authenticity. Third, we treat AUM as a retrieval index over a person rather than a prompt prefix, with a pipeline where a task classifier, component-selection function and budgeted retriever return a small payload of fields at generation time. Fourth, we evaluate it with sixteen language-model-simulated participants, six style-sensitive tasks and three seeds, plus a synthetic scaling study of the retriever. Retrieving eight fields matched the style fidelity of the full user model on 23% of the context (211 tokens against 915), improved on flat preference notes by 0.24 points on a five-point scale (p < 0.001, dz = 0.50), and raised forced-choice identification of the participant's own voice from 14.9% to 42.7% (25% chance). Four pre-registered controls returned null, locating the effect in the representation rather than the search over it. The benefit is largest for participants the un-personalised assistant reproduces worst (rho = -0.61, p = 0.013): personalisation is worth most to those the default serves least.

    无对称性的自旋劈裂：近补偿亚铁磁体与交替磁性的起源#
    Spin splitting without symmetry: a nearly compensated ferrimagnet and the origin of altermagnetism#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Joo Yull Rhee🔥 AI 10
    相关度
    9

    📄 摘要：共线交替磁体的自旋劈裂通常被归因于连接相反自旋子晶格的晶体对称性，即以旋转或镜面取代传统反铁磁体中的平移或反演。我们表明，这种对称性组织了劈裂，但并不产生劈裂；其起源是磁性轨道及其配体的各向异性排列，而这种排列也固定了对称性本身。两个结果证明了这一点。一个三斜（P1）ceMn 氧化物，即一个完全补偿的亚铁磁体，其两个不等价子晶格之间不存在任何对称性关系，却在整个布里渊区内发生自旋劈裂；将相同配体逐个原子重新定位，以恢复连接两个金属位点的反演中心，会使劈裂坍缩到小两个数量级的残余值，因此劈裂来自排列而非对称性。我们形式上表明，对称性永远只能反转或禁止一个已经存在的劈裂：它不能在某种排列已使其电子上相同的子晶格之间产生劈裂，也不能在底层轨道本身没有可供排列的各向异性时产生劈裂。通过一种按操作分辨的对称性层级组织分析，并将晶体类别解读为单一轴线，强制节点平面从正交、单斜到三斜类别依次由两个降为一个再到零，而由排列产生的劈裂持续存在到底层。交替磁体和完全补偿亚铁磁体因此是同一轴线的两端，即同一种劈裂在一端由对称性组织而在另一端未被组织；寻找补偿自旋劈裂磁体就是在轨道排列中搜索，而不是在对称性标签中搜索。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.12931v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The spin splitting of collinear altermagnets is usually attributed to the crystal symmetry relating the opposite-spin sublattices --- a rotation or mirror in place of the translation or inversion of a conventional antiferromagnet. We show that this symmetry organizes the splitting but does not produce it; its origin is the anisotropic arrangement of the magnetic orbitals and their ligands, which also fixes the symmetry itself. Two results establish this. A triclinic (P1) ceMn oxide, a fully compensated ferrimagnet whose two inequivalent sublattices are related by no symmetry, is spin split throughout the Brillouin zone; repositioning the same ligands, atom for atom, to restore an inversion centre relating the two metal sites collapses the splitting to a residual two orders of magnitude smaller, so the splitting follows from the arrangement rather than the symmetry. Symmetry, we show formally, can only ever reverse or forbid a splitting that already exists: it cannot create one between sublattices an arrangement has left electronically identical, nor where the underlying orbitals carry no anisotropy to arrange in the first place. Organizing the analysis through an operation-resolved symmetry hierarchy and reading the crystal classes as a single axis, the enforced nodal planes fall from two to one to zero across the orthorhombic, monoclinic, and triclinic classes while the arrangement-generated splitting persists to the base. The altermagnet and the fully compensated ferrimagnet are thus the two ends of one axis --- the same splitting, organized by symmetry at one end and unorganized at the other --- and the search for compensated spin-split magnets is a search over orbital arrangements rather than symmetry labels.

    💡 亮点：该研究挑战了将共线交替磁体自旋劈裂归因于连接相反自旋子晶格的晶体对称性的常见看法。作者认为，对称性组织劈裂但并不产生劈裂，其根源在于磁性轨道及其配体的各向异性排列。通过三斜 P1 补偿亚铁磁体以及恢复反演中心后的对比，研究指出寻找补偿自旋劈裂磁体应聚焦于轨道排列，而非仅依赖对称性标签。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与五位研究人员已有工作的直接连接是：磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构已经出现在团队的方向画像中，可对应到CrI₃/CrSBr 等范德华磁体、反铁磁/交替磁材料、磁性氧化物。与传统只做 0 K formation energy/静态势垒的工作相比，这里更应关注铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 晶体结构、功能材料和结构—性质关系在温度、缺陷、应变或外场下的变化；摘要没有给出的模型细节不作延伸判断。这种联系应通过同一材料体系上的独立基准和跨分布测试确认，不能仅凭方法名称或期刊来源下结论。

    💡 对你方向的启示：对当前研究最具体的启示不是泛泛地“使用 ML”，而是把该文的磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构嵌入现有材料模拟链：先在CrI₃/CrSBr 等范德华磁体、反铁磁/交替磁材料、磁性氧化物中选择一个已有 DFT 数据基础的体系，按“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”补充训练/验证集，再比较《无对称性的自旋劈裂：近补偿亚铁磁体与交替磁性的起源》对应模型对相稳定、极化/磁序、缺陷或动力学指标的改进。若结果只在训练分布内有效，应通过跨温度、跨应变、跨缺陷浓度和跨超胞测试检查可迁移性。第一步可以选取团队已有的 HfO₂、二维磁体、半导体缺陷或钙钛矿数据做小样本试验，再决定是否扩大到生产级计算。

    钙钛矿超晶格中二维平坦极性带产生的不可约亚平方纳米铁电畴#
    Irreducible sub-nm² ferroelectric domains by 2D-flat polar band in perovskite superlattices#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 铁电·极化📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Pawan Kumar, Jun Hee Lee🔥 AI 10
    相关度
    9

    📄 摘要：人们一直设想，铁电体中的平坦极性声子带能够实现不可约的小尺寸畴，从而达到最高的存储密度。然而，这类能带极其罕见；目前仅在铁电铪氧化物中发现了一维平坦极性带，并由此产生宽度为 2.7 埃、不可约的狭窄线型畴，即半个晶胞宽度。在这里，我们报道了在铁电 (BaTiO3)1/(BaXO3)1 超晶格（X = Zr、Sn）中发现二维平坦极性带。该平坦带在四分之一晶胞面积内产生高度局域且相互不作用的偶极子，并形成不可约亚平方纳米铁电畴的准简并态。估算的铁电畴密度超过 300 Tbit/cm2（假设每个畴对应一个比特），在已知铁电体中达到创纪录的高水平，同时这些偶极子能够在低电压下独立切换。承载二维平坦带的铁电相在超晶格的不同堆垛方向上构成竞争性基态，因此既可以在自由悬浮结构中实现，也可以通过在 MgO 衬底上外延生长实现。这一发现为探索铁电体中的多维平坦极性带以及设计超高密度、低功耗存储器件开辟了前所未有的机会。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.12329v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Flat polar phonon bands in ferroelectrics have been envisioned to realize irreducibly small domains for achieving the highest memory density. However, such bands are extremely rare, with only a one-dimensional flat polar band discovered in ferroelectric hafnia, which gives rise to irreducibly narrow, line-type domains with a half-unit-cell width of 2.7 Angstrom. Here, we report the discovery of a two-dimensional flat polar band in the ferroelectric (BaTiO₃)₁/(BaXO₃)₁ superlattice (X = Zr, Sn), which generates extremely localized, noninteracting dipoles within its quarter-unit-cell area that form quasi-degenerate states of irreducible sub-nm² ferroelectric domains. The estimated ferroelectric domain density, exceeding 300 Tbit/cm² (assuming one bit per domain) represents a record high among the known ferroelectrics, with independently switchable dipoles at low voltages. The ferroelectric phase hosting two-dimensional flat bands constitutes a competing ground state across superlattice stacking directions and thus can be experimentally realized in both freestanding structures and epitaxial growth on MgO substrates. This discovery opens unprecedented opportunities to explore multidimensional flat polar bands in ferroelectrics and to engineer ultra-dense and low-power memory devices.

    💡 亮点：研究发现，铁电 (BaTiO3)1/(BaXO3)1 超晶格（X = Zr、Sn）中存在二维平坦极性声子带，可在四分之一晶胞面积内形成高度局域且相互独立的偶极子。由此产生的准简并态对应不可约的亚平方纳米铁电畴，估算的铁电畴密度超过 300 Tbit/cm2，并可在低电压下独立切换。该铁电相在不同超晶格堆垛方向上都是竞争性基态，有望通过自由悬浮结构或在 MgO 衬底上的外延生长实现。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入HfO₂ 铁电、二维滑移铁电、极化翻转、畴壁和疲劳/缺陷问题。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 声子、有限温度和输运性质对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“把电场、极化、应变、氧空位和局域结构序参一起纳入轨迹数据；比较均匀翻转、局域成核和畴壁传播，并用 DFT/有效 Hamiltonian 对关键路径复核”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    文献研究：Hund’s physics-driven strong correlation in the altermagnet CaCrO3#
    Hund’s physics-driven strong correlation in the altermagnet CaCrO3#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 Preprints👤 Zhenfeng Ouyang, Peng-Jie Guo, Rong-Qiang He, Zhong-Yi Lu🔥 AI 10
    相关度
    9
    📖 英文原文

    Altermagnetism has attracted intense interest, but strong correlation effects in altermagnets remain largely unexplored. Here, using density functional theory plus dynamical mean-field theory (DFT+DMFT), we identify CaCrO3, a rare example of a metallic altermagnet, as a Hund altermagnet. We show that Hundness, rather than Mottness, leads to incoherent metallicity with altermagnetic spin splitting and sustains local spin moments. The DFT+DMFT calculated magnetic moment (~ 1.3 µB/Cr) is in quantitative agreement with neutron diffraction (~ 1.2 µB/Cr), while conventional DFT + U predicts an insulating state instead. Furthermore, Hundness promotes incipient flat bands and gives rise to heavy-fermion-like behavior in this 3d system, with effective mass enhancement m*/m ~ 47. This work demonstrates DFT+DMFT as a reliable computational tool for studying strongly correlated altermagnets and highlights the importance of Hund’s physics in these emerging materials.

    💡 亮点：论文结合 DFT+DMFT 研究金属交替磁体 CaCrO3，发现 Hund 相互作用而非 Mott 机制主导其强关联行为。该体系表现出交替磁自旋劈裂、局域磁矩、重费米子样有效质量增强和非相干金属性。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：密度泛函理论和电子结构计算，以及磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 晶体结构、功能材料和结构—性质关系对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与五位研究人员已有工作的直接连接是：磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构已经出现在团队的方向画像中，可对应到CrI₃/CrSBr 等范德华磁体、反铁磁/交替磁材料、磁性氧化物。与传统只做 0 K formation energy/静态势垒的工作相比，这里更应关注磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 晶体结构、功能材料和结构—性质关系, 非共线磁性、DMI/Kitaev 相互作用和交替磁性在温度、缺陷、应变或外场下的变化；摘要没有给出的模型细节不作延伸判断。这种联系应通过同一材料体系上的独立基准和跨分布测试确认，不能仅凭方法名称或期刊来源下结论。

    💡 对你方向的启示：对当前研究最具体的启示不是泛泛地“使用 ML”，而是把该文的磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构嵌入现有材料模拟链：先在CrI₃/CrSBr 等范德华磁体、反铁磁/交替磁材料、磁性氧化物中选择一个已有 DFT 数据基础的体系，按“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”补充训练/验证集，再比较《Hund’s physics-driven strong correlation in the altermagnet CaCrO3》对应模型对相稳定、极化/磁序、缺陷或动力学指标的改进。若结果只在训练分布内有效，应通过跨温度、跨应变、跨缺陷浓度和跨超胞测试检查可迁移性。第一步可以选取团队已有的 HfO₂、二维磁体、半导体缺陷或钙钛矿数据做小样本试验，再决定是否扩大到生产级计算。

    文献研究：Assessing the magnetic states and the accuracy of first-principles Hubbard corrections for the battery cathode LiₓCoO₂ (x=0,1)#
    Assessing the magnetic states and the accuracy of first-principles Hubbard corrections for the battery cathode LiₓCoO₂ (x=0,1)#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Valentina Sanella, Cristiano Malica, Alberto Carta, Maria Andolfatto, Nicola Marzari, Livia Giordano 等7位作者🔥 AI 10
    相关度
    8
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.12984v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: LiₓCoO₂ is a prototypical layered cathode material for Li-ion batteries, yet its accurate description from first principles remains challenging because of self-interaction errors, weak interlayer interactions, and a complex magnetic energy landscape. Here, we present a systematic investigation of the structural, electronic, magnetic, and electrochemical properties of LiₓCoO₂ (x=0,1) using density-functional theory augmented with self-consistent Hubbard corrections and long-range van der Waals interactions, together with a systematic exploration of possible magnetic states. The on-site interactions on Co-3d and O-2p states, as well as inter-site Co-O interactions, are determined from first principles using linear-response theory in the framework of density-functional perturbation theory, with Löwdin-orthogonalized atomic orbitals employed as Hubbard projectors. For LiCoO₂, the inclusion of Hubbard corrections provides an accurate description of the structural properties, while the electronic structure is very sensitive to the choice of Hubbard projectors. In particular, frontier Wannier-function projectors substantially improve the description of the occupied electronic states compared with localized atomic orbitals. For CoO₂, we demonstrate that a systematic exploration of the magnetic energy landscape is essential to identify the lowest-energy low-spin ground state. However, the resulting Hubbard-corrected electronic structure is insulating, consistent with the prediction of the HSE06 hybrid functional, but in contrast to the experimentally observed metallic behavior. Structural relaxation further drives the system toward a different metallic solution with an electronic configuration inconsistent with low-spin Co⁴⁺ character. Despite these limitations, the calculated intercalation voltages agree well with experiment, with deviations as small as 2%.

    💡 亮点：研究利用密度泛函理论结合自洽Hubbard修正和长程范德华相互作用，系统分析不同锂含量LiₓCoO2正极材料的结构、电子和磁性状态。结果表明，Hubbard投影子的选择会显著影响电子结构，前沿Wannier函数投影能够改善计算描述。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：本文系统比较LiCoO₂与脱锂CoO₂在不同磁态和Hubbard U处理下的结构、电子、磁性及电化学性质，重点评估第一性原理Hubbard修正能否同时描述层间作用、自相互作用误差、Co价态变化和复杂磁性能量面。技术路线应包括多种共线或非共线磁序、结构弛豫、U值扫描、可能的自旋轨道耦合以及形成能和电压比较；摘要未给出采用的交换—相关泛函、U确定方案、超胞大小和具体磁序清单。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与DFT+U、杂化泛函、线性响应U、团簇展开和自旋哈密顿量构建直接相关，也延续Ji-Hui Yang关于高脱锂LiCoO₂退化与电池界面的研究。Hongjun Xiang和Hongyu Yu的有效哈密顿量、复杂磁相互作用与机器学习势可用于把离散磁态扩展到大尺寸脱锂无序；Xin-Gao Gong关注缺陷、扩散和输运，可进一步处理氧空位与锂空位耦合。与RS-CIDER相比，本工作提供了强关联电池正极的关键基准。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可将LiₓCoO₂数据扩展为x=0、0.25、0.5、0.75、1及不同锂空位排布，使用线性响应U、HSE和实验晶格常数共同校准，并训练带磁性和化学环境输入的MACE或NEP势。随后以DFT+U标定Co—O交换、氧空位形成能、迁移势垒和局域自旋，结合团簇展开与蒙特卡洛预测温度和浓度效应；验证电压曲线、晶格参数、磁矩、XAS/中子可比特征及高脱锂相的结构稳定性。

    阅读原文 ↗

    文献研究：Laser-induced metastable (anti-)skyrmion states with higher-order topological charge#
    Laser-induced metastable (anti-)skyrmion states with higher-order topological charge#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Tim Titze, Marcel Möller, Timo Schmidt, Mariam Hassan, Sabri Koraltan, Stefan Mathias 等9位作者🔥 AI 10
    相关度
    8
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.13112v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Ultrafast creation and manipulation of topologically non-trivial spin objects promises significant potential for spintronic applications. Here, we demonstrate laser-induced nucleation of skyrmions and antiskyrmions with topological charge up to |Q|=3 from the saturated magnetization state of a Co/Ni-multilayer which does not intrinsically host a skyrmionic ground state. The resulting configuration of spin objects thus constitutes a metastable, thermodynamically hidden magnetic state. We control the number and type of spin objects by the excitation fluence and the external out-of-plane magnetic field.

    💡 亮点：研究利用激光在不具备本征斯格明子基态的 Co/Ni 多层膜中，诱导产生斯格明子和反斯格明子。通过调节激发光 fluence 和面外磁场，实现了拓扑荷最高达 |Q|=3 的亚稳态自旋结构调控。

    铁电—顺电相变过程中的异常相共存区间#
    Unusual phase coexistence regime across the ferroelectric - paraelectric transition#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 铁电·极化📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 M. Kopecký, J. Kub, E. de Prado, J. Hlinka🔥 AI 10
    相关度

    📄 摘要：研究人员通过 X 射线衍射，研究了生长在 DyScO3 衬底上的 PbTiO3 外延薄膜在 20–500 摄氏度温度范围内的性质。研究发现，铁电到顺电的相变经历了一个特殊的过渡性共存态，该状态延伸于 402 摄氏度和 414 摄氏度之间。在这一温区内、405 摄氏度处发生了畴结构转变，即三畴 c/a1/a2 多孪晶相转变为普通的 c/a2 孪晶相。研究提出，薄膜中的垂直应变梯度可能使两种相互独立的孪晶相与顺电相实现共存。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2609.12071v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: A PbTiO3 epitaxial thin film grown on a DyScO3 substrate was studied by x-ray diffraction in the temperature range of 20-500 degrees Celsius. It is found that the ferroelectric to paraelectric phase transition proceeds through a peculiar transitional coexistence state extending between 402 degrees Celsius and 414 degrees Celsius. Within this temperature interval, at a temperature of 405 degrees Celsius, a domain structure transition occurs, at which the three-domain c/a1/a2 polytwin phase transforms into an ordinary c/a2 twinned phase. It is proposed that the coexistence of two independent twinned phases with the paraelectric phase is enabled by vertical strain gradients in the film.

    💡 亮点：研究通过 X 射线衍射考察了生长于 DyScO3 衬底上的 PbTiO3 外延薄膜在 20–500 摄氏度范围内的结构变化。结果发现，铁电到顺电的相变经过 402–414 摄氏度的特殊过渡性共存态，并在 405 摄氏度发生畴结构转变。作者提出，薄膜中的垂直应变梯度可能使两种独立孪晶相与顺电相共存。

    文献研究：Hidden Crossover and Relaxor-Like Response from Emerging Polar Skyrmion Correlations in Ferroelectric Superlattices#
    Hidden Crossover and Relaxor-Like Response from Emerging Polar Skyrmion Correlations in Ferroelectric Superlattices#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Zhiyang Wang, Fei Yang, Long-Qing Chen
    相关度
    8
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2604.26100v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: Polar skyrmions in ferroelectric superlattices are nanoscale topological polarization textures typically regarded as weakly coupled objects confined to individual layers, with a role secondary to that of the underlying symmetry-breaking order parameter. Here using large-scale phase-field simulations of ferroelectric superlattices, we uncover a hidden thermal crossover deep inside the ferroelectric phase, where polar skyrmions evolve from an uncorrelated, layer-resolved state into an interlayer-correlated ensemble. This crossover occurs without additional symmetry breaking or a new order parameter, but produces a pronounced broad peak in the dielectric susceptibility. The anomaly originates from the competition between correlation-enhanced response, associated with the growth of interlayer skyrmion correlations, and polarization-induced stiffness, which suppresses dielectric fluctuations at low temperature. Under AC driving, the peak shifts with frequency, resembling relaxor ferroelectrics despite the absence of quenched disorder or polar nanoregions. Our results establish a disorder-free route to relaxor-like dielectric response and identify topological defect correlations as an organizing principle for thermodynamic anomalies, providing a mechanism distinct from conventional critical behavior associated with symmetry breaking and divergent order-parameter fluctuations.

    💡 亮点：该论文利用大规模相场模拟研究铁电超晶格中的极性斯格明子关联，发现铁电相内部存在隐藏热 crossover。该 crossover 使层内独立的极性斯格明子转变为层间关联态，并导致类似弛豫铁电体的介电响应峰。
